(Difficulty 69, formerly Difficulty 67) A question is asked: The Mu (front-alarm) points of the five Zang organs are all in the Yin region (explanatory note: chest, abdomen, and lateral costal region), while the Shu (back-transporting) points are in the Yang region (explanatory note: the Back, Foot Taiyang Bladder meridian). What does this mean? The answer is:

💡 术语解释：‘募穴’又称腹募穴，是脏腑之气汇集于胸腹部的穴位（如肺募中府 LU1、心募巨阙 CV14、肝募期门 LR14、脾募章门 LR13、肾募京门 GB25）；‘俞’指背俞穴，位于足太阳膀胱经第一侧线（背腰部，如肺俞 BL13、心俞 BL15、肝俞 BL18、脾俞 BL20、肾俞 BL23）。现代临床与解剖：募俞配穴是针灸经典配穴法，前募后俞对应胸腹与背部体壁节段，现代医学可从内脏-体壁反射、脊髓节段神经支配及自主神经调节加以理解，临床至今广泛应用。涉及穴位：中府（LU1）、巨阙（CV14）、期门（LR14）、章门（LR13）、京门（GB25）、肺俞（BL13）、心俞（BL15）、肝俞（BL18）、脾俞（BL20）、肾俞（BL23）。

阴病行阳。（背）阳病行阴。（腹）故令募在阴。（身以前）俞在阳。（身以后）

阴经有病会行于阳部（小注：背部），阳经有病会行于阴部（小注：腹部），所以使募穴位于阴部（小注：身体前部），背俞穴位于阳部（小注：身体后部）。

Diseases of Yin travel to the Yang region (explanatory note: the Back), and diseases of Yang travel to the Yin region (explanatory note: the Abdomen). Therefore, the Mu points are placed in the Yin region (explanatory note: front of the body) and the Shu points in the Yang region (explanatory note: back of the body).

💡 术语解释：‘阴病行阳’指里证、阴经病可反映或传及背部阳经；‘阳病行阴’指表证、腑病或阳热病可反映或传及胸腹阴部。医理与临床：体现了前后互应、阴阳相济的诊疗思路。在针灸临床和考试中，‘阳病行阴，阴病行阳’与募俞配穴密切相关，现代可从躯体反射区与内脏病变关联的角度理解。

(Difficulty 72, formerly Difficulty 71) A question is asked: What is meant by the classic's statement, 'When needling Ying (nutritive), do not injure Wei (defensive) (explanatory note: Ying is reached deeply, while Wei is reached superficially); when needling Wei, do not injure Ying (explanatory note: Wei focuses on releasing/regulating Qi, while Ying involves blood and bleeding)'? 💡 术语解释：‘营’指营气，行于脉中，与血关系密切；‘卫’指卫气，行于脉外，主司温煦与防御；‘刺荣无伤卫，刺卫无伤荣’出自《灵枢·终始》等篇，强调调理营卫层次时各守其分、互不干扰。现代临床与解剖：营卫分层可对应浅表皮下/微循环与较深层组织调节的层次差异，现代针刺注重进针深度控制与避免误伤血管，临床极具参考价值。

💡 术语解释：‘卧针’指针身与皮肤呈较小角度平刺或浅斜刺，以浅层调气为主；‘摄按’指押手按压、循按或散气手法；‘荣俞’指荥穴、俞穴等穴位。临床与现代对照：押手按压在现代针灸操作中具有减轻进针痛感、固定皮肤、辅助定位及调节局部气血的作用，‘刺卫无伤荣’提示浅表操作避免出血，‘刺荣无伤卫’提示深层操作注意层次与手法配合。

荣无伤卫。刺卫无伤荣也。

刺卫无伤营’。

and when needling Wei, do not injure Ying.'

💡 全篇总结：本难简明扼要地阐述了调理营卫深浅层次与押手配合的基本原则，是针灸基本功与手法层次观念的重要体现，现代临床仍具指导意义。

按灵枢曰。刺有三变。所谓刺荣卫。与寒痹留经是也。其言曰。刺营者出血。刺卫者出气。又素问曰。刺皮勿伤脉。

按《灵枢》说：刺法有三种变化，即所谓刺营卫、刺寒痹和刺留经。其中说：刺营分会使血液流出，刺卫分会使气宣散。又《素问》说：刺皮肤不要伤及血脉。

According to the Ling Shu, needling has three variations: needling ying and wei, needling cold bi-impediment, and needling lingering channels. It states that needling the ying draws blood, while needling the wei releases qi. Furthermore, the Su Wen states that needling the skin should not injure the blood vessels.

💡 术语：荣/营（营气，行于脉中，濡养全身）、卫（卫气，行于脉外，温煦防御）、寒痹（寒邪偏盛的痹证）、留经（邪气留滞经络）。医理：区分营卫与刺深浅，避免误伤气血。涉及穴位：无特定单一穴位代码。现代理解与对照：营卫与深浅层次对应体表与深层组织的解剖层次，现代针灸强调避开大血管与重要神经干。适用性：刺深浅与避害原则至今适用，但'刺营出血'在常规针灸中已较少用于常规补泻，多见于特定放血疗法。

刺脉勿伤肉。刺肉勿伤筋。刺筋勿伤骨。刺骨勿伤筋。刺筋勿伤肉。刺肉勿伤脉。刺脉勿伤皮。由此文而推之。所谓无

刺血脉不要伤及肌肉，刺肌肉不要伤及筋腱，刺筋腱不要伤及骨骼；反过来，刺骨骼不要伤及筋腱，刺筋腱不要伤及肌肉，刺肌肉不要伤及血脉，刺血脉不要伤及皮肤。由此推论，

Needling blood vessels should not injure the flesh; needling flesh should not injure the sinews; needling sinews should not injure the bone. Conversely, needling bone should not injure the sinews; needling sinews should not injure the flesh; needling flesh should not injure blood vessels; needling blood vessels should not injure the skin. '先虚后实'，是针灸家施补泻（给予与夺取）的法则。如果误用'先实后虚'，恐怕暗中耗散脱漏正气，所以告诫操作先后必须如此。

'First deficient, then excess' is the acupuncturist's principle of giving and taking. If one mistakenly does 'first excess, then deficient,' there is fear of covertly depleting and leaking zheng qi; therefore, the sequence is warned to be thus.

💡 术语：不得气、男外女内、十死不治（形容病情危重或邪正失调极深、预后不良的夸张说法）。医理：不得气提示经气未至或正气衰败，需调整层次再候气；'十死不治'属古籍夸张语。涉及穴位：无。现代理解与对照：针刺无任何酸麻胀重等'得气'反应可能提示局部感觉传导减弱、神经支配异常或全身状态极差；'十死不治'不能按字面理解为必然死亡。适用性：不得气需调整手法/选穴的思路实用，但'十死不治'在现代医学中仅为历史表述，不作预后定论。

💡 术语：厌按、弹而努之、爪而下之、直刺。医理：押手催气与进针衔接，得气后再行后续调气。涉及穴位：无。现代理解与对照：直刺适合肌肉较丰厚或需要垂直达一定深度的部位。适用性：基本进针法，至今适用。

Because tonification describes the method of inserting the needle but does not state how to withdraw it;

💡 术语：徐以深之（缓慢深刺）、动而伸之。医理：古籍校勘中指出文本结构可能存在详略或互文。涉及穴位：无。现代理解与对照：进针与出针速度、幅度会影响局部组织牵拉与痛觉感受。适用性：体现了古籍训诂考据视角，有助于理解原文行文特点。

泻既言出法。而不言内焉。由此考之。则补之出针。当不动而伸之。泻之入针。必当不须左手厌按之法。然则非有阙漏。

泻法既讲了出针的方法，却没有讲进针的方法。由此推考，那么补法出针时应当不摇动而直接出针，泻法进针时必定不需要左手按压催气的手法。这样看来，并不是有遗漏，

sedation states the withdrawal method but does not state the insertion method. Inferring from this, tonification should withdraw the needle without agitating it, while sedation surely need not use the left-hand pressing method upon insertion. Thus, there is no omission;

💡 术语：不动而伸之、互文。医理：通过对称推论补全补泻出入针的对应关系。涉及穴位：无。现代理解与对照：补法倾向温和缓和刺激，泻法倾向较强或直接疏散刺激。适用性：属于古注对文本逻辑的补足解释，供研读参考。

是指左手按压察见气已来到，于是进针；针刺入后，见到气（至）散尽再出针，这就叫做'有见如入，有见如出'。

means that the left hand (pressing) perceives qi arriving, and then the needle is inserted; once inserted, when the qi (arriving) subsides completely, the needle is withdrawn—this is called 'seeing as if entering' and 'seeing as if exiting.'

盖针法补之为难。故令王焘虞搏辈。发有泻无补之疑。且此篇于补一法。丁宁反复不止者。以其难故尔。于泻则略之。

大概因为针刺手法中补法最为困难，所以才让王焘、虞抟等人产生'只有泻法、没有补法'的怀疑；而且本篇对于补法反复叮咛不休，正是因为补法难的缘故，对泻法则写得简略，

Because among needling techniques tonification is the most difficult, it led figures like Wang Tao and Yu Tuan to raise doubts about whether there is 'sedation without tonification.' Moreover, this chapter repeatedly and earnestly dwells on tonification precisely because it is difficult, while treating sedation briefly

💡 术语：王焘（唐）、虞抟（明，底本作'虞搏'）、有泻无补之疑。医理：历史上有医家认为泻法易见效而补法难掌握。涉及穴位：无。现代理解与对照：在现代临床研究中，'补'与'泻'的客观特异性仍存在探讨空间，强刺激（常被类比为泻）往往比温和刺激更容易引起明显的即时感觉或反应。适用性：反映针灸史上对补泻难易与客观性的长期争论，具有学术史价值。

💡 术语：昼夜五十度（营气昼夜循行五十周的传统计数）、荣卫流行。医理：强调气血循环往复的节律性。涉及穴位：无。现代理解与对照：'五十度'是古代时间与循环节律的象征性量化表述，现代生理学以心率、心输出量及昼夜节律衡量循环与节律。适用性：作为经典循环节律理论了解，不作字面定量生理指标。

漏刻计时与呼吸次数而运行；而且卫气白天行于各阳经，夜间行于各阴经，这就叫做'荣卫流行'。手三阳经从手走向头，足三阳经从头走向足，手三阴经从胸腹走向手，

(Difficulty 76, formerly 79) Question: The classic (Ling Shu, Chapter 1) says, 'Greeting and taking it away (sedation)—how could there not be depletion (of pathogenic qi)? Following and aiding it (tonification)—how could there not be fullness (of upright qi)?'

💡 术语：迎而夺之、随而济之（《灵枢·九针十二原》语）。医理：泻法旨在夺去邪气使邪虚，补法旨在济助正气使正实。涉及穴位：无。现代理解与对照：可对应降低过度亢进/痛觉敏化状态与增强功能低下/代偿状态。适用性：针灸补泻核心理论之一，教学与考试高频考点。

与分歧互有出入，不能一概而论，所以说'实与虚，是牢与濡的意思'。所谓'牢'代表邪气实，那么'濡'就代表正气虚；'濡'代表邪气虚，那么'牢'就代表正气实，这是互文表达的。

and divergences are not fixed; hence it says, 'Excess and deficiency mean firm and soft.' What is meant by 'firm' as pathogenic excess means 'soft' is upright deficiency; 'soft' as pathogenic deficiency means 'firm' is upright excess—expressed through mutual implication.

💡 术语：虚者补之、实者泻之、不虚不实、以经取之（取本经腧穴调理经气）。医理：确立虚补、实泻、不虚不实取本经的三类基本针刺原则。涉及穴位：无特定穴位代码。现代理解与对照：可类比为对功能不足者增强刺激、功能亢进者抑制/缓解、局部功能紊乱但无明显全身虚实偏盛者直接调理对应局部/节段相关穴位。适用性：针灸治疗总则与考试高频考点，至今高度适用。

虚者补其（经）母。（穴）（邪）实者泻其（经）子（穴）当先补之。然后泻之。（前言先补后泻之意）不虚不实。以经取

虚的，就补其所属经脉的'母'穴；邪气实的，就泻其所属经脉的'子'穴；应当先补后泻（重申前文先补后泻之意）。至于'不虚不实，以经取之'，

is deficient, tonify its channel's 'mother' (point); when (pathogenic qi) is excess, sedate its channel's 'child' (point); one should tonify first and then sedate afterward (reiterating the earlier principle of tonifying before sedating). As for 'neither deficient nor excess, treat it through the channel itself,'

💡 术语：补其母、泻其子、以经取之。医理：虚实夹杂先补后泻，非虚非实直取本经。涉及穴位：无特定穴位代码。现代理解与对照：根据病程与体质选择循经远近配穴或局部取穴。适用性：经典配穴与补泻原则，临床与考试均具重要价值。

之者。是正（本）经自生。病不中他（经）邪也。当自取其经。（本经所属穴）故言以经取之。

是指病邪由本经自身产生，并没有受到其他经脉邪气的影响，应当自行取用本经的穴位来治疗，所以说'以经取之'。

it means the disorder arises within the channel itself and is not struck by pathogenic qi from another channel; one should select points from that channel itself—hence it says 'treat it through the channel itself.'

💡 术语：正经自生、不中他邪、自取其经。医理：本经自病重在疏通调理本经经气。涉及穴位：无。现代理解与对照：类似于局限性、原发性局部经络/神经肌肉功能失调，直接选取该经循行部位穴位进行局部或循经调节。适用性：本经取穴原则在现代针灸临床极为常用。

按此承前篇。再发问答。以断灵素所言。不虚不实。以经取之之语。审其立问之意。子母补泻之义。前既已悉。

按：这是承接前篇再次提出问答，来阐明《灵枢》《素问》所说的'不虚不实，以经取之'这句话。审察提问的本意，子母补泻的道理前面已经详尽说明，

Note: This follows the previous chapter to pose another question and answer, clarifying the statement from the Ling Shu and Su Wen: 'Neither deficient nor excess, treat it through the channel itself.' Examining the intent of the question, the meaning of mother-child tonification and sedation was already thoroughly explained earlier,

💡 术语：审其立问之意、子母补泻。医理：梳理前后篇分工与逻辑衔接。涉及穴位：无。现代理解与对照：无。适用性：文献研读与教学参考。

言则非所发。疑唯议补泻之外。别有经刺一法耳。盖补法为随。泻法为迎。若夫以经取之。则非刺子母而刺属己者。

这句话不是为再讲子母补泻而发的，估计只是在补泻之外，另外提出一种'经刺'的方法罢了。大凡补法为'随'，泻法为'迎'；至于'以经取之'，则是不用母子穴而刺本经所属穴位。

so the statement was not raised for that purpose; it is likely proposing a 'channel-needling' method apart from tonification and sedation. Generally, tonification is 'following' and sedation is 'greeting'; as for 'treating it through the channel itself,' one needles points belonging to the channel itself rather than mother or child points.

💡 术语：经刺、属己者（本经所属穴）。医理：区分母子补泻与本经直接取穴两种选穴思路。涉及穴位：无。现代理解与对照：本经取穴更侧重沿该经循行路径或病变所在经段施治。适用性：临床选穴思维辨析。

且夫谓母能令子虚。则补母者。治其本也。其病从母及子也。谓子能令母实。则泻子者治其末也。其病从子加母也。

况且说'母能令子虚'，那么补母穴就是治其'本'，这是病邪从母经传及子经的情况；说'子能令母实'，那么泻子穴就是治其'末'，这是病邪从子经传加到母经的情况。

Moreover, saying 'the mother can make the child deficient' means tonifying the mother treats the 'root,' where the illness spreads from mother to child; saying 'the child can make the mother excess' means sedating the child treats the 'branch,' where the illness extends from child to mother.

💡 术语：母能令子虚、子能令母实、治其本、治其末。医理：以五行母子传变解释虚实与补泻先后。涉及穴位：无。现代理解与对照：体现整体关联与因果链条思维，但五行生克非现代病理生理机制。适用性：中医基础理论与针灸辨证常用分析框架。

是皆他邪所为者尔。正经自病者。本经之气失常。则流行错乱。故用针治其经气而已。是其非有虚。又非有实。有何迎随之施

这些都是其他经脉邪气所致；至于正经自病，是本经气机失常而致循行错乱，所以只需用针调治本经之气就行了，这既不是虚，也不是实，哪里还要施用迎随补泻呢？

These are all caused by pathogenic qi from other channels; as for a channel falling ill on its own, the qi of that channel itself loses normalcy and circulates chaotically, so one merely uses the needle to regulate its channel qi—this is neither deficiency nor excess, so what occasion is there to apply greeting and following?

💡 术语：流行错乱、治其经气。医理：强调本经自病重在理气通经，而非套用子母补泻。涉及穴位：无。现代理解与对照：局部神经肌肉或循环失调重点在于恢复局部正常功能与传导节律。适用性：有助于避免生搬硬套五行补泻，临床具参考价值。

哉。余观本邦近之世。用针治病。率皆经刺一法。而未尝闻有全行迎随子母法。况若前诸篇所载。取五输法。亦唯廑廑参星。

呢？我看近代我国（作者指日本江户至近代）用针治病，大都采用'经刺'这一种方法，未曾听说有全面施行迎随子母法的；至于前面各篇所记载的取五输穴之法，也只是寥寥无几、如晨星般稀少，

Indeed! Observing how needling is used to treat illness in our country in recent times, it mostly relies on the single method of 'channel needling,' and I have never heard of fully practicing the mother-child greeting-and-following method; even more so, the methods of selecting five-shu points recorded in earlier chapters are few and scattered like sparse stars,

💡 术语：本邦近世（此处为《难经古义》作者滕万卿视角，指日本江户时代）、经刺、五输法（井、荥、俞、经、合五输穴）。医理：记述特定历史时期与地域针灸流派的 临床现状。涉及穴位：五输穴（井、荥、俞、经、合，泛指各经特定穴类）。现代理解与对照：不同国家与流派在针灸临床上各有侧重，现代针灸则融合多流派经验与循证评估。适用性：具有针灸流派史与文献史料价值。

殆几乎熄。悲夫。古昔圣贤。苦口丁宁。垂教万世。徒存方策。被蠹鱼害。噫。

几乎快要湮没失传了。可悲啊！古代圣贤苦口婆心、谆谆教诲以垂训万世，如今却空留书简，遭受书虫蛀蚀，唉！

almost extinguished. Alas! The sages of antiquity earnestly and repeatedly imparted teachings for ten thousand generations, yet they remain merely in books, gnawed by bookworms—alas!

💡 术语：方策（书简、典籍）、蠹鱼（蛀虫，比喻岁月侵蚀或典籍失修）。医理：感叹古籍经典传承不易。涉及穴位：无。现代理解与对照：无。适用性：体现传统学者对典籍保存与研读的珍视。

（第七十八旧七十五）难曰。经言东方实。（言肝重实）西方虚。（言肺重虚）泻南方。（心从肝有余）补北方。（肾

（第七十八难，旧本为第七十五难）问：经书上说'东方实（指肝气偏实），西方虚（指肺气偏虚）；泻南方（心火随肝木有余而旺），补北方（肾

(Difficulty 78, formerly 75) Question: The classic says, 'The east is excess (referring to liver qi being markedly excess) and the west is deficient (referring to lung qi being markedly deficient); sedate the south (as heart fire is excess following the liver) and tonify the north (as kidney

💡 术语：东方实/西方虚（五行方位配五脏，东属木配肝、西属金配肺）、泻南方/补北方（南属火配心、北属水配肾）。医理：以五行生克与方位配属阐述肝木乘脾/侮金或心火亢盛、肾水不足的调治逻辑。涉及穴位：无特定穴位代码。现代理解与对照：五行方位配脏腑是古代系统类比模型，现代医学不以地理方位或五行生克解释脏腑病理生理。适用性：中医五行学说与针灸辨证基础理论，考试与研读常见，需结合现代医学客观认识。

从肺不足）何谓也。然。金木水火土。当更相平。（举五行相制常例）东方（肝）木也。西方（肺）金也。木欲实。金当平之。（金克木）火欲实。水当平之。（水克火）土欲实。木当平之。（木克土）金欲实。火当平之。（火克金）水欲实。土当平之。（土克水）东方肝也。则知肝实。（非等闲实）西方肺也。则知肺虚。（非寻常虚）泻南方（心）火。补北方（肾）水。南方火。火者木之子也。北方水。水者木之母也。（二句起下病因与治法要语）子（心）能令母（肝）实。母（肾）能令子（肝）虚。水胜火。（一句处方大关系旧本误出于前故今移此）故泻（心）火补（肾）水。欲令（肺）金不得平（肝）木也。经曰。不能治其虚。（治如是虚者非通变则不能）何问其余。（他病）此之谓也。

（承接上文『从肺不足』而来）这是什么意思呢？回答说：金、木、水、火、土五行，应当相互制约以维持平衡。（这是列举五行相制约的一般规律）东方属肝，在五行属木；西方属肺，在五行属金。肝木将要亢盛，肺金应当制约它。（这是金克木）心火将要亢盛，肾水应当制约它。（这是水克火）脾土将要亢盛，肝木应当制约它。（这是木克土）肺金将要亢盛，心火应当制约它。（这是火克金）肾水将要亢盛，脾土应当制约它。（这是土克水）东方对应肝，由此可知肝木实盛；（这里的『实』不是一般的实）西方对应肺，由此可知肺金虚弱。（这里的『虚』不是寻常的虚）治疗时采取泻南方心火、补北方肾水的方法。南方属火，火是木的子；北方属水，水是木的母。（这两句话引出下文关于病因和治法的关键论述）心火过亢能使肝木实盛，肾水充足能使肝木之气平抑（或转虚/受制于母）。『水胜火』这句话对处方立法至关重要，旧刻本误放在前面，所以现在移到这里。因此泻心火、补肾水，是想让肺金不必直接去平抑肝木。医经上说：『不能治好它的虚证，还去问什么其他病症呢？』（要治好这种虚证，若不懂得变通权变就做不到）说的就是这个道理。

What does this mean (following from 'stemming from lung deficiency')? It is thus: Metal, wood, water, fire, and earth ought to mutually restrain one another to maintain balance. (This states the general rule of Five Phases restraint.) The east corresponds to the liver and is wood; the west corresponds to the lung and is metal. When wood tends toward excess, metal should restrain it. (Metal conquers wood.) When fire tends toward excess, water should restrain it. (Water conquers fire.) When earth tends toward excess, wood should restrain it. (Wood conquers earth.) When metal tends toward excess, fire should restrain it. (Fire conquers metal.) When water tends toward excess, earth should restrain it. (Earth conquers water.) The east is the liver, so we know the liver is in excess (not an ordinary excess); the west is the lung, so we know the lung is deficient (not a common deficiency). Drain southern (heart) fire and tonify northern (kidney) water. The south is fire, and fire is the child of wood; the north is water, and water is the mother of wood. (These two sentences introduce the key principles of pathogenesis and treatment below.) A child (heart) can cause the mother (liver) to become excessive, and a mother (kidney) can make the child (liver) deficient/restrained. 'Water overcomes fire' (this single sentence has major bearing on prescription design; older editions misplaced it earlier, so it is moved here). Thus, draining heart fire and tonifying kidney water is intended so that lung metal does not have to directly restrain liver wood. The classics say: 'If one cannot treat its deficiency, what else is there to ask about?' (To treat such a deficiency requires flexible adaptation.) This is what is meant.

💡 【术语与医理】五行相制（相克）：木克土、土克水、水克火、火克金、金克木，维持动态平衡；『子能令母实』『母能令子虚』在此结合『泻南补北』『水胜火』阐述变通治法（注：传统五行生克中常言『母虚子实』『子盗母气』，此处按原文语境解为心火亢盛逆制/助长肝木亢盛，补肾水以滋水涵木、济火制亢）。【涉及穴位】本段未指定具体穴位；临床若依滋水涵木、泻心火调肝肾思路，可参考肾经太溪 KI3、照海 KI6、复溜 KI7，心经少府 HT8、神门 HT7，肝经太冲 LR3、行间 LR2，肺经太渊 LU9、尺泽 LU5。【今天是否适用及原因】五行生克制化是中医系统论与脏腑相关思维模型，今天仍用于指导辨证思路与整体调理，但不可机械套用五行符号代替客观病理。【现代医学对照】体现多器官/系统之间的功能代偿与失衡连锁反应（如神经-内分泌-免疫调节与肝、肺、心、肾代谢与循环的联动），但五行并非解剖学或生理学实体。【考试/临床意义】《难经》临证变通思维的代表段落，提醒临床遇虚实夹杂、母子相涉之病不可死守『虚则补其母、实则泻其子』或单一克制法，须审察标本缓急。

按东实西虚。即谓肝木实。肺金虚。皆是病之所在焉。泻南补北。即谓泻心火。补肾水。皆是治之所归焉。此乃发难之端也。答辞先举五行相制者。表其常例。火者木之子。水者木之母二句。此篇一大关系。乃树下文分病因与治法之帜。盖肝之亢极。本因心火有余。子有余则不食母气。肝木所以盛实。肾之衰竭。原关肺金不足。母不足则无助子气。肺金所以太虚。以五行相制之常。为之治则。当补肺泻肝而平之。今乃弃东西而治南北者。非经常之法。犹之儒家有权。兵法有奇乎。所谓子能令母实一句。言病因子者心。母者肝。母能令子虚一句。言治法。母者肾。子者肝。或问如果其说之是。则心之有余。既令肝实。则肝之有余。亦当令肾实。然则肾胡为虚乎。曰心令肝实者。其气逆而为邪也。夫木生火者。顺道也。今心有余。而不食母气。故木气不达而反逆。所以溯洄为邪焉。肾之为虚。既失母气。夫水生木者。亦顺道也。虽然。其气不足。故将通于彼。则不可以逮。何逆流之有。且自心而传于肝者。邪气也。从肾而通于肝者。正气也。辟诸水流。其末窒碍不通。则逆。逆则贲激。激则混浊。本源为之沸腾。是岂水之性哉。肝之所以有实也。源既细微。则其流不长。纵使堤防在其下流。势已微。则不能达中道而涸。遂委泥沙。何奔逆之有。肾之所以有虚也。或又问泻火补水。此两药并行乎。将所谓先补后泻邪。曰否。苟以针石言之。即应补阴泻阳而可。若夫汤液。则不必然。唯其补阴是务。观水胜火一句。可以见已。是亦此篇一大要语。不可忽略。熟察答意。此证原因。中气虚而脾不能散精。上归于肺。肺乏主气。此肺一虚。肺气不行。则肾阴不足。阴不足。则阴中之阳动焉。故肝木逆上。并于心。此肝一实。心气有余已极。则不食母气。而传道不通。肝邪益炽。此肝重实。心气有余。则上克肺。此肺重虚。至其施治。则肺虚者。置而不取。辟犹齐问菁茅。讨南巡而崇衰周之朝焉。补肾则阴气自盛。而心失其势。取援于母。则肝实日减。辟犹晋假道于虞以代虢。其实则晋不在虢而在虞焉。所谓欲令金不得平木。及不能治其虚。何问其余。是此之谓欤。余释此难之义。旁通陈廷芝王安道孙一奎三子之说。各有所取舍。盖历代诸家。纷纷未有定论。王氏当近之世。勃然独得此篇之旨。然至于以不一字为衍。则千虑一失。实可惜乎。陈孙二氏。存不字以立其义。则独得其本旨。何者。此篇本论应变之治。故从旁补其不足。一举以立两全之功。若八十篇所言。则补肺泻肝。直取其常制之法。固有彼此常变之分。若以去不字。则与下编混为一意。岂合扁鹊之旨乎。学人审诸。

按语说：『东实西虚』，就是指肝木实盛、肺金虚弱，这都是病变所在的部位。『泻南补北』，就是指泻心火、补肾水，这都是治疗所归结的法度。这是提出发问的开端。回答先举出五行相制约的规律，是表明常规法则。『火是木的子』『水是木的母』这两句话，是这一篇的关键所在，用来确立下文分别论述病因与治法的纲领。大凡肝气亢盛到极点，根本原因是心火有余；子气有余而不去承接消耗母气，所以肝木就盛实起来。肾气衰竭，原本与肺金不足有关；母气不足就无力资助子气，所以肺金就非常虚弱。按照五行相制约的常规作为治则，应当补肺泻肝来使它平复；如今却舍弃东西（肺与肝）而去治理南北（心与肾），这不是常规治法，就像儒家讲的『权变』、兵法讲的『奇兵』一样。所谓『子能令母实』一句，是说致病因素中，『子』指心，『母』指肝；『母能令子虚』一句，是说治疗法则中，『母』指肾，『子』指肝。有人问：如果这种说法是对的，那么心火有余既然能使肝木实盛，肝木有余也应当使肾水实盛，那么肾为什么反而虚弱呢？回答说：心使肝实，是因为其气逆行而成为邪气。木生火是顺应常道的，如今心火有余却不接纳消耗母气，于是木气不能畅达而反逆，因此逆流而成为邪气。肾之所以虚弱，是因为失去了母气（肺金之气）的资助；水生木虽然也是顺应常道，但是肾气本身不足，想要通达于肝却力不逮，怎么会有逆流冲激的现象呢？况且从心传到肝的是邪气，从肾通到肝的是正气。好比水流，它的末梢阻塞不通就会倒流，倒流就会奔腾激荡，激荡就会混浊，源头也随之沸腾，这难道是水本来的特性吗？这就是肝之所以有实邪的原因。水源既然细微，水流就不会长远；纵使下游有堤防，水势已经微弱，未到半途就会干涸，于是沉积泥沙，哪里会有奔腾逆流呢？这就是肾之所以有虚弱的原因。又有人问：泻火补水，这两种药物是同时并用呢，还是所谓先补后泻呢？回答说：不是这样的。如果从针灸砭石来说，应当补阴泻阳就可以了；至于汤药，就不一定如此，唯有以补阴为要务，看『水胜火』这句话就可以明白了。这也是这一篇的一大要旨，不可忽视。细细体会回答的意思，这种病证的原因是：中气虚弱，脾不能输布精微向上归于肺，肺缺乏主气的功能；一旦肺虚，肺气不能运行，就会导致肾阴不足；阴液不足，阴中之阳就会躁动，所以肝木逆上而并入于心。一旦肝实，心气有余到了极点，不再承接母气，传化通道阻塞，肝邪就更加炽盛，这是肝的『重实』；心气有余就会向上克伐肺金，这是肺的『重虚』。至于施治方法，对于肺虚，暂时搁置不直接去补，好比齐桓公借问包茅贡品，以讨伐南巡为名而尊崇衰微的周王室一样；补肾就能使阴气自然旺盛，心火就会失去气势，向母气寻求援助，肝实就会日渐减轻，好比晋国向虞国借道去讨伐虢国，实际上晋国的意图不在于虢国而在于虞国。经文所说的『欲令金不得平木』，以及『不能治其虚，何问其余』，大概说的就是这个意思吧？我阐释这一难的含义，旁通了陈廷芝、王安道、孙一奎三家的说法，各有取舍。大抵历代各家众说纷纭，还没有定论。王安道在近世能够勃然独得这一篇的宗旨，然而至于把『不』字当作衍文，则是千虑一失，实在可惜啊！陈、孙二家保留『不』字来确立它的含义，才真正得其本旨。为什么呢？这一篇本来论述的是应变的治法，所以从旁补其不足，一举建立两全的功效；至于第八十难所说，则是补肺泻肝，直接采取常规制约的治法，本来就有常与变的分别。如果去掉『不』字，就与后文混为一谈，哪里符合扁鹊的宗旨呢？学医的人应当审慎明察啊！

Commentary: 'East excess and west deficiency' refers to liver wood being in excess and lung metal being deficient—both are where the disease lies. 'Drain south and tonify north' refers to draining heart fire and tonifying kidney water—both are where the treatment is directed. This is the starting point of the question. That the answer first cites Five Phases restraint shows the standard rule. The two phrases 'fire is the child of wood' and 'water is the mother of wood' are pivotal to this chapter, establishing the banner for distinguishing pathogenesis and treatment method below. Generally, extreme liver hyperfunction originally stems from excess heart fire; when the child has excess and does not consume/draw upon the mother's qi, liver wood becomes over-abundant. Kidney depletion originally relates to insufficient lung metal; when the mother is deficient and cannot assist the child's qi, lung metal becomes severely deficient. By the regular rule of Five Phases restraint, one should tonify the lung and drain the liver to balance them; now, abandoning east and west to treat north and south is not a routine method, much like expedient adaptation (quan) in Confucianism or unorthodox tactics (qi) in military strategy. In 'a child can cause the mother to become excessive,' regarding pathogenesis, 'child' refers to the heart and 'mother' to the liver. In 'a mother can make the child deficient/restrained,' regarding treatment, 'mother' refers to the kidney and 'child' to the liver. Someone asks: If this theory is right, then since excess heart fire makes the liver excessive, excess liver should also make the kidney excessive—so why is the kidney deficient instead? Answer: When the heart makes the liver excessive, its qi rebels and becomes pathogenic. Wood generating fire follows the normal order; now heart fire is in excess yet does not take in the mother's qi, so wood qi cannot flow smoothly and rebels instead, surging backward as a pathogen. The kidney is deficient because it has lost the support of the mother's qi (lung metal). Water generating wood is also the normal order, yet its qi is insufficient, so when trying to reach there it falls short—how could there be surging backflow? Moreover, what transmits from heart to liver is pathogenic qi, whereas what flows from kidney to liver is upright/healthy qi. Compare it to flowing water: if its downstream outlet is blocked, it flows backward; backing up causes surging, surging causes turbidity, and the source boils over—is that the natural state of water? That is why the liver has excess. When the source is thin and weak, the stream does not run far; even if there is a dam downstream, the momentum is already weak and dries up mid-course, depositing silt—how could there be rushing backflow? That is why the kidney is deficient. Someone further asks: When draining fire and tonifying water, are these two medicinals used concurrently, or is it a matter of tonifying first and draining afterward? Answer: Not necessarily so. Speaking of needling and stone probes, tonifying yin and draining yang suffices. As for herbal decoctions, it is not necessarily so; rather, tonifying yin is the primary task—seeing the line 'water overcomes fire' makes this clear. This is also a major tenet of this chapter and must not be overlooked. Carefully examining the intent of the reply, the cause of this condition is: middle qi is deficient, the spleen cannot distribute essence upward to the lung, and the lung lacks its governing qi function. Once the lung is deficient and lung qi fails to circulate, kidney yin becomes insufficient; when yin is insufficient, yang within yin stirs, so liver wood rebels upward and merges with the heart. Once the liver is in excess, heart qi excess reaches an extreme, fails to draw upon mother qi, and transmission is obstructed, making liver evil even fiercer—this is 'double excess' of the liver. Excess heart qi then rebels upward to conquer lung metal—this is 'double deficiency' of the lung. As for treatment, for the lung deficiency one sets it aside and does not directly tonify it, much like the state of Qi demanding the overdue tribute of bundled reeds and launching a southern expedition while honoring the declining Zhou court. Tonifying the kidney allows yin qi to flourish on its own, depriving the heart of its momentum; seeking aid from the mother gradually reduces liver excess, much like Jin borrowing a passage through Yu to attack Guo—in reality Jin's aim was not Guo but Yu. The phrases 'intending so that metal does not have to restrain wood' and 'if one cannot treat its deficiency, what else is there to ask about' refer to this. In explaining the meaning of this difficulty, I have cross-referenced the views of three masters—Chen Tingzhi, Wang Andao (Wang Lü), and Sun Yikui—weighing what to adopt and discard. Across dynasties, commentators have held conflicting views without a settled conclusion. Wang [Andao] in recent times suddenly grasped the core intent of this chapter on his own, yet treating the character 'bu' (not) as a scribal error is a slip in a thousand considerations, truly regrettable. Chen and Sun preserved 'bu' to establish its meaning, thus grasping the true original intent. Why? This chapter originally discusses expedient/adaptive treatment, so it tonifies deficiency from a flanking angle to achieve a two-fold benefit at once. As for what is discussed in Chapter 80, tonifying the lung and draining the liver directly applies the regular restraining method; there is inherently a distinction between normal rules and adaptive changes. If one deleted 'bu,' it would merge into the same meaning as the later section—how would that match Bian Que's intent? Students of medicine should examine this carefully.

💡 【术语与医理】《难经古义》作者滕万卿（日本江户时代）的按语。核心在辨析『常』与『变』：常规肝实肺虚宜『培土生金、金平木』（补肺泻肝），而本难『泻南补北』『补肾水』属『权变之法』（滋水涵木、壮水制火），并考辨『不』字存废（注者赞成陈廷芝、孙一奎存『不』字，批评王安道删『不』字）。文中提及『脾不能散精上归于肺』及『重实』『重虚』链条。【涉及穴位】无明文穴位；调补肾阴、滋水涵木常涉太溪 KI3、照海 KI6、三阴交 SP6、肝俞 BL18、肾俞 BL23。【今天是否适用及原因】医家考据与『常变权变』临床思维今天仍有很高参考价值；至于『水枯无逆流』『堤防』等比喻，属于古人说理譬喻，需结合现代病理生理学理解。【现代医学对照】可类比慢性肝病、循环/体液失衡或应激状态下多系统相互影响（如肝-肾-心-呼吸轴的复杂代偿失调），但不能将『子盗母气』『水胜火』等同于具体器官疾病机制。【考试/临床意义】中医基础理论与针灸临床中『虚则补其母、实则泻其子』并非绝对机械公式，需结合脏腑生克乘侮与标本缓急灵活运用，切忌生搬硬套。

（第七十九旧七十七）难曰。经（逆顺篇）言上工治未病。（邪未传处）中工治已病（病既所在）者。何谓也。然。所谓治未病者。见肝之病。则知肝当传之（指邪）与脾。故先实其脾气。（邪将传）无令得受肝之邪。（将克之势）故曰治未病焉。中工治已病者。见肝之病。不晓相传。（邪气）但一心治肝。（病所在）故曰治已病也。

（第七十九难，旧本排在第七十七难）问：医经（《灵枢·逆顺》篇）上说：『上等医术高明的医生治疗未发生的病（指邪气尚未传到的脏腑），中等水平的医生治疗已经发生的病（指邪气已经所在的脏腑）。』这是什么意思呢？回答说：所谓『治未病』，是见到肝脏有病，就知道肝邪按照五行规律应当传给脾，所以先充实脾气（邪气将要传到的脏腑），不让它受到肝邪的侵袭（木克脾的趋势），所以叫做『治未病』。所谓『治已病』，是见到肝脏有病，不懂得脏腑之间病邪相传的规律，只是一心一意去治疗肝（病邪所在的部位），所以叫做『治已病』。

(Seventy-ninth Difficulty, formerly Seventy-seventh) Question: The classic (Lingshu, 'Ni Shun', Counterflow and Compliance) states: 'The superior physician treats disease before it arises (where the pathogen has not yet spread), while the mediocre physician treats disease that has already manifested (where the disease already resides).' What does this mean? Answer: What is meant by 'treating disease before it arises' is that upon seeing disease in the liver, one knows that the liver's pathogenic influence will by rule transmit to the spleen; therefore, one first fortifies the spleen qi (which is about to receive the pathogen) so that it does not succumb to the liver's pathogen (the tendency of wood conquering earth). Hence this is called 'treating disease before it arises.' 'Treating disease that has already manifested' means that upon seeing disease in the liver, one does not understand the transmission of pathogens among viscera and focuses solely on treating the liver (where the disease already is). Hence this is called 'treating disease that has already manifested.'

💡 【术语与医理】『治未病』是中医预防与截断传变的核心思想，此处具体指『既病防变』——见肝之病，知肝传脾，当先实脾。依据五行『木克土』规律，肝病易乘脾，提前健脾扶正可阻断病邪传变。【涉及穴位】原文无指定穴位；临床『见肝之病，知肝传脾，先实其脾气』常配伍健脾和胃穴如足三里 ST36、脾俞 BL20、章门 LR13（肝之募、脾之会）、太白 SP3，配合疏肝穴如太冲 LR3、期门 LR14。【今天是否适用及原因】高度适用。『既病防变、阻断传变』是当代慢病管理、康复医学与针灸临床的核心策略。【现代医学对照】对应现代医学中的『二级预防』与『早期干预/阻断并发症进展』，如慢性肝病患者早期注意营养支持、胃肠功能维护及门脉高压/代谢并发症的预防。【考试/临床意义】中基、针灸学高频考点。『见肝之病，知肝传脾，当先实其脾气』不仅是预防观念，也提示临床治肝常需兼顾脾胃，防止木克土致纳呆、腹胀、泄泻。

按谓未病者。指其所未受邪。已病者。指已所病。凡诸脏病动辄传其所克。假令上工治未病。则当先望视其横与纵之所在。预防其蚕食。盖有绸缪牖户之渐焉。中工治已病。无有远虑。胡祛近患。坐执一故尔。灵枢第五十五篇云。上工刺其未生者也。下工刺其方袭者也。此篇之旨。率由斯文。素问第二篇所言。未病已病。义与此异。彼谓未病者。指无病患。言不可混同。前篇所谓泻南补北。是上工之治未病也。故承上而言。上中二工。各有阶级也。

按语说：所谓『未病』，是指尚未受到病邪侵害的脏腑；所谓『已病』，是指已经发生病变的脏腑。凡是各脏发生病变，往往容易传变到它所克制的脏腑。假使高明医生『治未病』，就应当先观察病邪横向传克或纵向传生的趋势所在，预防它像蚕食桑叶那样逐步侵蚀，这大概有未雨绸缪、修补门户的深意吧。中等医生『治已病』，缺乏长远谋划，怎能消除即将发生的祸患，只是拘泥于一点一隅罢了。《灵枢》第五十五篇（《逆顺》）说：『上等医生针刺尚未萌生的病邪，下等医生针刺刚刚侵袭的病邪。』这一篇的宗旨，大多源于这段经文。《素问》第二篇（《四气调神大论》）所说的『不治已病治未病』，含义与此不同，那里说的『未病』是指还没有生病的人，二者的说法不可混同。前一篇所说的『泻南补北』，也是高明医生『治未病』的做法，所以承接上文而论述，上工与中工各自有不同的层次境界。

Commentary: What is called 'not-yet-diseased' refers to viscera that have not yet received the pathogen; 'already-diseased' refers to what has already become ill. Whenever any organ develops disease, it readily transmits to the organ it conquers/restrains. Suppose a superior physician treats disease before it arises: one should first observe where lateral (conquering) or longitudinal (generating) transmission is heading, preventing it from advancing like a silkworm nibbling mulberry leaves—this carries the gradual foresight of repairing windows and doors before rain falls. A mediocre physician treating already-manifest disease lacks far-sighted planning; how can they avert impending trouble when rigidly fixated on a single point? Lingshu Chapter 55 ('Back and Forth') says: 'The superior physician needles what has not yet come into being; the inferior physician needles what is just attacking.' The main idea of this chapter largely derives from this passage. What Suwen Chapter 2 ('Great Treatise on Regulating the Qi in Accordance with the Four Seasons') says about 'not-yet-diseased' and 'already-diseased' differs in meaning from this: there, 'not-yet-diseased' refers to people who are not yet ill, and the two meanings must not be conflated. 'Draining the south and tonifying the north' mentioned in the preceding chapter is also a way for a superior physician to treat disease before it arises; thus, continuing from above, superior and mediocre physicians each have their own distinct levels.

💡 【术语与医理】明确区分两个经典文献的『治未病』内涵：《灵枢》及本难侧重『既病防变』（已病脏腑防传变），《素问·四气调神大论》『圣人不治已病治未病』侧重『未病先防』（健康人群养生防病）。并提及『横』（相克传变，如肝传脾）与『纵』（相生传变，如母子相及）及『蚕食』之象。【涉及穴位】原文无指定穴位；临床调理脾胃、预防肝邪乘脾常用足三里 ST36、中脘 CV12、公孙 SP4、阴陵泉 SP9。【今天是否适用及原因】文献辨析与『未病先防+既病防变』双轨预防理念在今天极为适用，是中医治未病学科的理论基石。【现代医学对照】『未病先防』对应一级预防（健康促进、疫苗与生活方式干预）；『既病防变』对应二级/三级预防（早诊早治、防恶化与并发症）。【考试/临床意义】常见易混考点：切忌把《素问》第二篇与《灵枢》/《难经》第七十九难的『治未病』混为一谈。

（第八十旧八十一）难曰。经言无实（指真）实（指假）虚（指真）虚。（指假）损不足而益有余。（谓治之害）是寸口脉（见之）邪。将病（证）自（然）有虚实邪。其损（不足）益（有余）奈何。然。是病（二字滑注以为衍孙一奎断为古言之法今从孙氏）非谓寸口脉也。（在脉自见其真而不见其假）谓病自其（假）虚（假）实也。假令肝（真）实而肺（真）虚。肝者木也。肺者金也。金木当更相平。（是言常例）当知金平木。（是言常治法）假令肺（真）实而肝（真）虚。（此肺动辄见假虚肝见假实）用针不补其肝。（真虚）微少气。（三字旧本误在用针上今考文义以移诸此）而反重实其肺。（真实）故曰实实虚虚。损不足而益有余。此者中工之所害也。

（第八十难，旧本排在第八十一难）问：医经上说：'不要使实证更实、虚证更虚，不要损伤不足的而增益有余的'（这是指治疗不当造成的祸害），这是就寸口脉而言呢，还是说疾病（证候）本身自有虚实呢？其中的损与益是怎么回事？回答说：『是病』这两个字（滑寿注认为是衍文，孙一奎断定为古人修辞句法，今从孙氏之说），并不是说寸口脉象（脉象上往往能显现出真情，而不易显现假象），而是说疾病证候本身存在着真假虚实。假使肝是真亢盛而肺是真虚弱，肝属木，肺属金，金木应当相互制约平衡，就应当知道用肺金平抑肝木（这是常规治法）。假使肺是真实盛而肝是真虚弱（这种情况下肺往往显现出假虚象，肝往往显现出假实象），用针刺治疗时不补益真虚的肝，『微少气』这三字（旧本误排在『用针』上面，今根据文义移到这里），反而进一步加重实邪所在的肺，这就是所谓的『实实虚虚，损不足而益有余』，这是中等水平医生造成的治疗之害。

(Eightieth Difficulty, formerly Eighty-first) Question: The classic says: 'Do not make the excessive more excessive or the deficient more deficient; do not damage the insufficient and augment the surplus' (referring to harm caused by treatment). Does this refer to the cun-kou (radial) pulse, or does the illness (its manifestations) itself have excess and deficiency? How do the damaging and augmenting come about? Answer: The two words 'is disease' (which Hua Shou's commentary regarded as redundant, while Sun Yikui judged to be an archaic linguistic construction—here we follow Sun) do not refer to the cun-kou pulse (which generally reveals the true state rather than the false), but rather mean that the illness itself has false deficiency and false excess. Suppose the liver has true excess and the lung has true deficiency: the liver is wood and the lung is metal; metal and wood should mutually restrain and balance one another, so one should know to use metal to restrain wood (the routine treatment method). Suppose the lung has true excess and the liver has true deficiency (in such cases the lung readily displays false deficiency and the liver displays false excess): if one needles without tonifying the truly deficient liver—the three characters 'wei shao qi' ('slightly deficient qi,' misplaced above 'needling' in older editions and moved here by textual meaning)—and instead further augments the truly excessive lung, this is what is called 'making the excessive more excessive and the deficient more deficient, damaging the deficient and augmenting the excessive.' This is the harm caused by the mediocre physician.

💡 【术语与医理】『实实虚虚，损不足而益有余』指因误诊误治导致虚者更虚、实者更实。本难探讨『真虚假实』与『真实假虚』的辨别，强调『不补其肝而反重实其肺』的误治后果（补泻颠倒、虚实错认）。【涉及穴位】原文无指定穴位；临床若涉及肝虚补肝、肺实泻肺，可参考肝经曲泉 LR8、阴包 LR9、行间 LR2，肺经尺泽 LU5、孔最 LU6、列缺 LU7。【今天是否适用及原因】『真虚假实、真实假虚』及『实实虚虚』的警示今天仍极具临床指导意义，尤其在针灸补泻手法与方药配伍中防止误补误泻。【现代医学对照】可对应临床误诊误治导致的病情加重（如对功能亢进/代偿期误行抑制或对虚弱状态误行强刺激/过度干预），但『实实虚虚』属于中医辨证论治与治疗学范畴。【考试/临床意义】常见考题，重点理解『损不足而益有余』并非疾病自然转归必然如此，多因辨证失误、治法悖逆所致。

按病有虚实。虚实有真假。其真焉者。合于脉象而知之。故亡论已。至于假焉。则中工之徒。动误其诊。故引经言戒之曰。无实实虚虚。言勿以假为真而治焉。若有实实虚虚。而真假相错。则不足者愈损。有余者愈益。若夫寸口脉。则虚自见虚。实自见实。故鲜有失诊之误矣。故曰非谓寸口之脉也。谓病自有虚实也。所谓虚实者。言假虚假实也。若肝实肺虚。是真虚实。故直行补肺泻肝之道。肺实肝虚。即在脉。则当见其真。苟在证。则多见假者。何则。肺主敛。其脏嫩软。虽病有实。见证多似不足。肝主发生。其脏猛烈。虽病有虚。见证多似有余。故中工误认。以假实为真。以真实为假。徒使病者受医之桎梏。非正命而死焉。悲夫。因前既尤中工粗无远略。故重深戒之。此难与七十八篇所云。东实西虚。固有常变之分。义互相发。不宜为一例看。

按语说：疾病有虚有实，虚实之中又有真有假。真正属实的虚实，结合脉象就能知晓，所以不必多议论。至于虚假的虚实，中等水平的医生往往容易诊断失误，因此引用经文告诫说：『不要使实者更实、虚者更虚』，意思是不要把假象当作真相来治疗。如果出现『实实虚虚』，真假交错，那么本来不足的就更加受损，本来有余的就更加亢盛。至于寸口脉象，虚就显现为虚，实就显现为实，所以很少出现诊脉失误的情况。因此说不是指寸口脉，而是说疾病证候本身有虚实。这里所说的虚实，是指『假虚』和『假实』。如果是肝真亢盛、肺真虚弱，这是真虚实，就直接采用补肺泻肝的方法。如果是肺真亢盛、肝真虚弱，在脉象上应当显现出它的真象，但如果看外在证候，却多显现出假象。为什么呢？因为肺主收敛，其脏质地娇嫩柔软，即使有实邪，表现出来的证候也多像不足（假虚）；肝主升发疏泄，其脏刚猛暴烈，即使有虚损，表现出来的证候也多像有余（假实）。所以中等医生容易误认，把假实当作真实，把真实当作虚假，白白让病人遭受庸医治疗的束缚，甚至不寿而死，真是可悲啊！因为前面已经批评中等医生粗疏缺乏长远谋略，所以这里再次深切告诫。这一难与第七十八难所说的『东实西虚』，本来就有常与变的分别，含义可以相互发明，不宜当作同一类情况来看待。

Commentary: Illness has excess and deficiency, and within excess and deficiency there is true and false. True excess and deficiency can be known by correlating with the pulse, so there is little need to dwell on them. As for false manifestations, mediocre practitioners frequently misdiagnose them; hence the classic warns against 'making the excessive more excessive and the deficient more deficient,' meaning do not treat false appearances as if they were true. If 'making the excessive more excessive and the deficient more deficient' occurs with truth and falsehood entangled, then what is deficient becomes even more damaged and what is excessive becomes even more exaggerated. As for the cun-kou pulse, deficiency naturally shows as deficiency and excess naturally shows as excess, so diagnostic errors there are rare. Thus it says it is not speaking of the cun-kou pulse, but that the clinical presentation itself has excess and deficiency—here meaning 'false deficiency' and 'false excess.' If there is true liver excess and true lung deficiency, one directly applies the method of tonifying the lung and draining the liver. If there is true lung excess and true liver deficiency, the pulse should show the true state, whereas external symptoms often show false appearances. Why? Because the lung governs astringency/descent and is a delicate, soft viscus, so even when excess is present, the manifested signs often resemble deficiency (false deficiency); the liver governs rising/free flow and is vigorous and fiery, so even when deficiency is present, the manifested signs often resemble excess (false excess). Therefore mediocre physicians misidentify false excess as true, and true as false, causing patients to suffer under misguided treatment and die prematurely—how lamentable! Because the preceding section already faulted mediocre physicians for coarseness and lack of foresight, this gives a deep, renewed warning. This chapter and Chapter 78's 'east excess and west deficiency' inherently have a distinction between normal rule and adaptive variation; their meanings illuminate one another and should not be treated as identical cases.

💡 【术语与医理】提出『肺主敛，其脏嫩软，虽病有实，见证多似不足；肝主发生，其脏猛烈，虽病有虚，见证多似有余』的脏腑特性假象论：肺气郁滞或实邪阻滞可致气短息微似虚（大实有赢状），肝阴肝血虚损、阳气升散失制可致急躁易怒、头胀眩晕似实（至虚有盛候）。【涉及穴位】原文无指定穴位；临床辨明真假后，肝虚宜滋养肝血肝阴（如太冲 LR3、三阴交 SP6、肝俞 BL18、血海 SP10），肺实宜宣肺降气清泻（如尺泽 LU5、列缺 LU7、肺俞 BL13）。【今天是否适用及原因】『大实有赢状、至虚有盛候』的辨证思维在临床上十分实用，能避免单凭表面症状（如烦躁、乏力、气促）草率用药或进针。【现代医学对照】可类比疾病表现与潜在病理生理状态不一致的情况（如重症感染/休克或代谢紊乱时表现出的复杂代偿或假性缓解/假性亢进），需结合客观检查综合判断。【考试/临床意义】辨别『真虚实』与『假虚实』的核心在于『四诊合参、脉证合参』，不可只看表面症状便下补泻结论。

（第八十一旧十二）难曰。经（灵枢第一第三）言五脏脉（气）已绝于内。（尺）用针者。反实其外。（阳经之合）五脏脉（气）已绝于外。（寸）用针者反实其内。（阴经之合）内外之绝。何以别之。然。五脏脉（气）已绝于内（下部）者。肾肝气已绝于内也。（下焦所治）而医（下工）反补其心肺。（无虚地）五脏脉（气）已绝于外（上部）者。其心肺脉已绝于外也。（上焦所治）而医反补其肾肝。（无虚地）阳绝补阴。阴绝补阳。是谓实实虚虚。损不足益有余。（儆戒之辞）如是死者。医杀之耳。

（第八十一难，旧本排在第十二难）问：医经（《灵枢》第一篇《九针十二原》、第三篇《小针解》）上说：『五脏的脉气已经在内（尺部）断绝，用针的人反而去补益它在外的阳经合穴；五脏的脉气已经在外（寸部）断绝，用针的人反而去补益它在内的阴经合穴。』内绝和外绝应当怎样区分呢？回答说：五脏脉气在内（下部，寸口尺部）断绝的，是肾气、肝气在内断绝（属于下焦所主），而医术低下的医生反而去补益它的心肺（心肺并没有虚损）；五脏脉气在外（上部，寸口寸部）断绝的，是心脉、肺脉在外断绝（属于上焦所主），而医术低下的医生反而去补益它的肾肝（肾肝并没有虚损）。阳绝补阴、阴绝补阳，这就叫做『实实虚虚，损不足而益有余』（这是警诫之词），像这样导致病人死亡的，是医生害死了他啊！

(Eighty-first Difficulty, formerly Twelfth) Question: The classics (Lingshu Chapter 1, 'Nine Needles and Twelve Source Points,' and Chapter 3, 'Minor Needle Explanations') state: 'When the pulse (qi) of the five viscera is already cut off internally (in the chi/cubit region), the needler instead reinforces its external aspect (the he-sea points of yang meridians); when the pulse (qi) of the five viscera is already cut off externally (in the cun/inch region), the needler instead reinforces its internal aspect (the he-sea points of yin meridians).' How are internal and external cutoff distinguished? Answer: When the pulse/qi of the five viscera is cut off internally (lower portion / chi pulse), it is kidney and liver qi cut off internally (pertaining to the lower burner), yet an inferior physician instead tonifies the heart and lung (where there is no deficiency). When the pulse/qi of the five viscera is cut off externally (upper portion / cun pulse), it is heart and lung pulse cut off externally (pertaining to the upper burner), yet an inferior physician instead tonifies the kidney and liver (where there is no deficiency). Tonifying yin when yang is cut off, or tonifying yang when yin is cut off, is called 'making the excessive more excessive and the deficient more deficient, damaging the deficient and augmenting the excessive' (a cautionary warning). When patients die thus, it is the physician who kills them.

💡 【术语与医理】本难源于《难经》第十二难（旧本误置），讨论寸口脉『内绝』（尺脉气绝，注者解为肝肾下焦之气虚绝）与『外绝』（寸脉气绝，注者解为心肺上焦之气虚绝）及针刺误治之害。注意：『绝』在危重病脉诊中常提示脏气衰败、预后极差，此处『反补其心肺/肾肝』指不分虚实、错补无虚之脏的逆治之弊。【涉及穴位】原文提到了『阳经之合』（足三里 ST36、上巨虚 ST37、下巨虚 ST39、委中 BL40、阳陵泉 GB34、小海 SI8 等）与『阴经之合』（尺泽 LU5、曲泽 PC3、少海 HT3、阴陵泉 SP9、曲泉 LR8、阴谷 KI10），作为针刺取穴分类概念，临证须辨明脏腑虚实与标本。【今天是否适用及原因】脉气『已绝』在现代临床多属危重症或濒死状态，针灸仅可作辅助急救或对症支持，绝不可夸大疗效或盲目补泻；但『辨明虚实、对证施治、戒盲目乱补』的临床安全底线今天完全适用。【现代医学对照】脉气断绝、寸尺失常在现代医学中可对应严重循环衰竭、休克、心律失常或终末期多器官功能衰竭；医疗差错或不当干预加重病情则对应医源性损害（iatrogenic harm）。【考试/临床意义】针灸临床安全核心准则：严禁在未明确辨证、脏腑虚实不清时滥用补泻针法，危重病人须及时转诊或中西医协同救治，切忌延误抢救。

按灵枢第三篇云。五脏之气。已绝于内者。脉口气内绝不至。反取其外之病处。与阳经之合。有留针以致阳气。阳气至则内重竭。重竭则死矣。五脏之气。已绝于外者。脉口气外绝不至。反取其四末之输。有留针以致其阴气。阴气至。则阳气反入。入则逆。逆则死矣。张会卿曰。脉口浮虚。按之则无。是谓内绝不至。脏气之虚也。脉口沉微。轻取则无。是谓外绝不至。阳之虚也。此篇问答。盖据此文变例。而发其余蕴。灵枢所谓内绝者。指阴经之虚。外绝者。指阳经之虚。故内绝取阳经之合。外绝取四末之输。是乃议阴虚补阳。阳虚补阴之误也。素问云。治脏者治其俞。治腑者治其合。是正道也。而今治脏反取阳合。治腑反取阴俞。其误可知矣。扁鹊所谓内外绝者。舍腑不论。偏举脏而言之。故知外绝者。心肺之虚。而寸脉浮虚内绝者。肾肝之虚。而尺脉沉微。是为异也。下工动辄有此反治。故重深戒之曰。如此死者。医杀之耳。前篇既举中工之害。则此言医者。当指下工。旧本误出于第十二篇。冯氏谓此篇合入用针补泻之类。当在六十难之后。以例相从也。今从其说。以类移于此云。

按语说：《灵枢》第三篇（《小针解》）说：『五脏之气已经在内断绝的，是脉口之气在内断绝不能到达，反而去取它在外的病处以及阳经的合穴，留针来招致阳气；阳气一到，内里就更加枯竭，更加枯竭就会死亡。五脏之气已经在外断绝的，是脉口之气在外断绝不能到达，反而去取它四肢末端的输穴，留针来招致阴气；阴气一到，阳气反而内入，内入就会气机逆乱，气机逆乱就会死亡。』张会卿说：『脉口浮取虚弱，按之却没有，这叫做内绝不至，是脏气虚弱；脉口沉细微弱，轻取却没有，这叫做外绝不至，是阳气虚弱。』这一篇的问答，大概是根据《灵枢》这段文字的变例，而发挥出其余的深意。《灵枢》所说的『内绝』，是指阴经的虚损；『外绝』，是指阳经的虚损。所以内绝却取阳经的合穴，外绝却取四肢末端的输穴，这就是阴虚补阳、阳虚补阴的错误做法。《素问》说：『治疗脏病的取其俞穴，治疗腑病的取其合穴。』这才是正道；如今治脏却反而取阳经合穴，治腑却反而取阴经输穴，其错误就很清楚了。扁鹊所说的『内外绝』，舍弃六腑不论，专门举五脏来说，所以知道『外绝』是指心肺之虚，而寸脉浮虚；『内绝』是指肾肝之虚，而尺脉沉微，这就是与《灵枢》原意不同的地方。低下的医生往往有这种颠倒错乱的治疗，所以再次深切告诫说：『像这样死亡的，是医生害死了他啊。』前一篇已经列举了中等医生的危害，那么这里所说的『医』，应当是指更低一层的『下工』。旧刻本误放在第十二难，冯氏认为这一篇应当归入针刺补泻一类，放在第六十难之后，按同类体例归附，如今采纳他的说法，按类别移到这里。

Commentary: Lingshu Chapter 3 ('Minor Needle Explanations') states: 'When the qi of the five viscera is cut off internally, the qi of the pulse-mouth is cut off internally and does not reach; instead taking the affected site externally and the he-sea points of yang meridians, and retaining the needle to summon yang qi—when yang qi arrives, the interior is doubly depleted, and double depletion brings death. When the qi of the five viscera is cut off externally, the qi of the pulse-mouth is cut off externally and does not reach; instead taking the shu-stream points at the four extremities, and retaining the needle to summon yin qi—when yin qi arrives, yang qi instead enters inward, entering causes reversal, and reversal brings death.' Zhang Huiqing said: 'When the pulse-mouth is floating and hollow/empty, and absent on pressing, this is called internal cutoff without arrival, indicating deficiency of visceral qi; when the pulse-mouth is deep and faint/minute, and absent on light touch, this is called external cutoff without arrival, indicating deficiency of yang.' The Q&A in this chapter likely takes a variant reading of that passage and develops its remaining implications. In the Lingshu, 'internal cutoff' refers to deficiency of yin meridians, and 'external cutoff' refers to deficiency of yang meridians; thus taking yang he-sea points for internal cutoff and extremity shu-stream points for external cutoff is the error of tonifying yang in yin deficiency and tonifying yin in yang deficiency. The Suwen says: 'For treating zang organs, treat their shu-stream points; for treating fu organs, treat their he-sea points.' That is the proper path; now treating zang organs by taking yang he-sea points, and treating fu organs by taking yin shu-stream points—its error is evident. When Bian Que speaks of internal and external cutoff, he sets aside the fu organs and speaks specifically of the zang organs; thus we know 'external cutoff' refers to deficiency of heart and lung (with a floating-empty cun pulse), while 'internal cutoff' refers to deficiency of kidney and liver (with a deep-faint chi pulse)—this is where the difference lies. Inferior physicians often commit such inverted treatments, hence the deep, renewed warning: 'When patients die thus, it is the physician who kills them.' Since the preceding chapter already presented the harm done by mediocre physicians, 'physician' here should refer to the inferior practitioner (xia-gong). Older editions misplaced it at Difficulty 12; Master Feng suggested this chapter belongs with needling tonification and drainage and should sit after Difficulty 60 by topical alignment. Here we follow his view and move it here by category.

💡 【术语与医理】此处注者梳理了《灵枢·小针解》《素问》与《难经》对『内绝』『外绝』及『俞穴』『合穴』的差异：五输穴中『输』属土（五行配属中输穴多与脾土/经气注输相关，原文引《素问》『治脏者治其俞，治腑者治其合』，注：常见经典表述为『荥输治外经，合治内腑』，此为注者引述文献之文），『合』属水，主气聚入深处。文末体现文献考据与篇目编排意识（上工/中工/下工分级）。【涉及穴位】涉及『阳经之合』（如足三里 ST36、阳陵泉 GB34、委中 BL40）与『四末之输』（如太渊 LU9、太白 SP3、神门 HT7、太冲 LR3、太溪 KI3 等十二经输穴类属）。【今天是否适用及原因】五输穴『合治内腑』等临证配穴规律在今天针灸临床仍广泛使用；但将『脉绝』绝对化为单一脉象预后判断，现代临床需结合生命体征与客观检查，不可仅凭切脉断生死。【现代医学对照】『脉气绝而不至』类比严重循环衰竭、休克或心肺功能濒临衰竭；强调临床操作规范与危重病人转诊原则，避免误治加重病情。【考试/临床意义】针灸学五输穴应用及『治分深浅、辨证施治』的典型文献对比，考试中需注意不同古籍对同一术语的语境差异。

○余缵前修之业。自壮岁时讲究此书。业已数百遍。至今三十年所。无日不钻研。古人云。读书百遍。其义自通。不佞如万卿。虽未曾中其肯綮。然且莫寓意。以俟左右逢其原之日久矣。顾其距春秋时邈焉。则其言亦渊乎深哉。故其历世所注传。自吴吕广至明吴文。凡十有九家。愈繁愈杂。辟犹百川派分。无繇寻源。于是舟之方之。渐得观溯洄之澜。以问渤海之津。尝读韩非子说林。其中有言秦荆有。荆人傍说晋叔向。叔向论城壶丘可否。以令二国和焉。余读

○我继承前辈先贤的学术事业，自从壮年时期钻研《难经》这本书，已经读了数百遍，至今大约三十年，没有一天不潜心研读。古人说：『读书百遍，其义自通。』像我万卿这样不才的人，虽然未必每次都能切中要害，但也一直寄托心意、耐心等待『左右逢其原』（融会贯通、触类旁通）的那一天，已经很久了。只是这本书距离春秋时期十分遥远，它的言辞也深邃渊博啊。因此历代对它的注释传本，从三国吴地的吕广到明代的吴文，一共有一十九家，越发繁多驳杂，好比百川分流支散，没有门径去寻找源头。于是我驾舟行船、循序探求，渐渐得以看见溯流而上的波澜，从而探问通往渤海的渡口。我曾经读过《韩非子·说林》，其中讲到秦国与楚国有争端（原文『秦荆有』疑脱漏『构兵/争』等字），楚国人顺势游说晋国的叔向，叔向评论修筑壶丘城池的可行与否，从而促使两国和解。我读到——

Commentary / Postscript: I have carried on the scholarly legacy of earlier masters. Since my prime years I have studied this book hundreds of times; for roughly thirty years up to now, not a day has passed without diligent inquiry. As the ancients said, 'Read a book a hundred times and its meaning becomes self-evident.' Though an unaccomplished person like myself, Wanqing (Teng Wanqing), has not always hit upon its vital core, I have set my mind upon it and awaited the day of 'meeting the source at every turn' for a long time. Yet because it is far removed from the Spring and Autumn period, its words are profoundly deep indeed. Thus the commentaries handed down through the ages—from Lü Guang of Wu to Wu Wen of the Ming, nineteen commentators in all—have grown ever more numerous and entangled, like a hundred rivers branching out with no path to trace the source. Thereupon I set boat and raft to explore, gradually coming to see the surge upstream and inquire at the ford of the Bohai Sea. I once read the 'Shuolin' ('Forest of Persuasions') chapter of the Han Feizi, which contains an account concerning Qin and Chu (Jing), where a man of Chu side-persuaded Shu Xiang of Jin, and Shu Xiang discussed whether fortifying Huqiu was advisable so as to bring the two states into peace. When I read —

💡 【术语与医理】此处为《难经古义》作者滕万卿自述治学经历与注疏考据方法。『左右逢其原』出《孟子·离娄下》，意为取之左右皆逢其源，形容学问融会贯通、得心应手；『肯綮』指筋骨结合处，比喻要害。作者列举自吴吕广至明吴文十九家注《难经》，并借《韩非子·说林》寓言说明旁通博采、化解纷争与贯通义理的治学态度。结尾'余读'接下一段'至是'。【涉及穴位】无穴位。【今天是否适用及原因】『读书百遍，其义自通』与循源溯本、博采众长而不盲从的治学精神今天依然适用；但具体历史注家数量及春秋扁鹊成书年代属于医史文献考据范畴。【现代医学对照】不涉及现代医学解剖或疾病机制；可类比循证学习与系统梳理文献演变脉络的方法论。【考试/临床意义】了解《难经》流传注疏史（如吕广、丁德用、滑寿、王九思、孙一奎等）有助于把握不同医家诠释视角，临证则须回归临床实践检验理论。

至是。喟然叹曰。呜呼。扁鹊设难之意。于叔向乎尽矣。无乃刻意叔向。以体扁鹊乎。乃余所注解。有取乎尔。亦有取

——到这里，我禁不住长叹一声说：啊！扁鹊设立八十一难的深意，在（春秋晋国贤大夫）叔向的言论精神中已经体现得淋漓尽致了啊！难道不是（后世注家或我本人）刻意去效法、揣摩叔向的微言大义，以此来体悟扁鹊的本意吗？于是我所作的这些注解，正是有所取法于此（叔向之义），也有所取法于彼（扁鹊之旨）啊。

At this point, I heaved a deep sigh and said: 'Alas! The profound intent behind Bian Que's formulation of the Difficult Issues is completely exhausted in the wisdom of Shu Xiang! Is it not by deliberately emulating Shu Xiang that one comes to embody the true spirit of Bian Que? Therefore, the annotations I have composed draw both from the former and from the latter.'

💡 【文献背景】此段是《难经古义》作者滕万卿（日本江户时代医家）书末题记的结尾，下句'乎尔'接于后。【术语解释】叔向：春秋晋国大夫羊舌肸，以博学善辞著称。作者借《韩非子·说林》所记叔向化解秦楚争端的故事，说明自己注书意在调和诸家分歧、回到扁鹊本意。未涉及具体腧穴。

乎尔。

💡 【文献说明】此二字为上段末尾“亦有取 / 乎尔。”因古籍排版换行而断开的残句，在翻译与研读时须与上文连读，“乎尔”为文言句末语气助词，表肯定与感叹。

跋 ## Postscript

难经古义。家大人历年讲书之暇。折中诸家。独断为说。先是庚辰之春。将竣上木之事。不图罗祝融氏之灾。半已

《难经古义》这部书，是家父（滕万卿）多年来在讲授医书的闲暇时间里，折中调和各家注解的得失，凭藉自己独到的见解撰写而成的。在此之前，庚辰年（1760年，日本宝历十年）的春天，书稿即将完成刻版印刷的工作，不料却遭遇了火灾（祝融氏之灾），书稿与雕版有一半都化为了乌有。

The 'Ancient Meanings of the Classic of Difficulties' (Nan Jing Gu Yi) was written by my father during his leisure time from lecturing on medical texts over the years, harmonizing the views of various commentators and forming his own independent interpretations. Earlier, in the spring of the Gengchen year (1760), just as the woodblock carving for printing was about to be completed, a fire disaster unexpectedly struck, reducing half of the work to ashes.

💡 【术语解释】1. 家大人：对他人称自己父亲的敬辞，此处指《难经古义》作者滕万卿（日本江户时代医家）。2. 折中诸家，独断为说：指广泛参考历代《难经》注本（如滑寿《难经本义》、丁德用、吕广等），去伪存真，提出独立见解。3. 上木：古时书籍印刷需将文字刻于木板上，故称刊印出版为“上木”或“付梓”。4. 祝融氏之灾：祝融为古代神话中的火神，“祝融氏之灾”即火灾的雅称。【文献价值】交代了《难经古义》初稿在宝历十年（1760年）毁于火灾的曲折成书史，有助于中医古籍版本学与目录学考证。

乌有。嗣后稍稍起草。今北复旧。然大人为业。奔走四方。不遑宁处。故门人泉春安再三校正。不佞仲实与焉。

（化为）乌有。此后家父又一点点重新起草增补，如今才得以恢复原貌（注：原文“北”当为“比”或“始”之形讹，意为“近来/才”）。然而家父因为行医济世的职业，常年奔走于四方诊治病人，没有时间安闲地停下来（亲自校勘），所以由门生泉春安反复校对订正，没有才能的我（仲实）也参与了这项校勘工作。

(Reduced) to nothing. Thereafter, my father gradually drafted the text anew, and only recently has it been restored to its former state. However, because my father's medical practice required him to travel constantly in all directions with no leisure to rest in one place, his disciple Izumi Shunan repeatedly proofread and corrected the manuscript, and I, his untalented son Zhongshi, also participated in the work.

💡 【文字校勘】原文“今北复旧”中的“北”字，结合文意与古籍刻本常见讹误，应为“比”（近来、等到）或“始”（方才）的形近之误。【文化与中医传承】1. “奔走四方，不遑宁处”反映了古代临床医家以行医治病为第一要务，著作多在临床间隙与师徒授受中完成。2. “门人……再三校正”体现了传统中医“师带徒”模式下，弟子通过参与典籍校勘、整理师说来深化对经典的掌握。3. “不佞”为古代男子的谦称，意为没有才能。

检阅已竣。乃请父执山公介净书。剞劂告成。因述其事。以谢迟滞之罪云。

全书审阅校对完毕后，于是请父亲的老友山公介用工整的楷书誊清底稿，随后雕刻印刷正式告成。特此记述这段经过，以对本书出版延误之过表示歉意。

Once the inspection and proofreading were completed, we invited my father's close friend, Shan Gongjie, to write out a clean fair copy, and the woodblock engraving was finally accomplished. Therefore, I have recounted these events to apologize for the long delay in publication.

💡 【术语解释】1. 父执：父亲的挚友。2. 净书：古籍刻版前，请书法工整者将校定稿誊写成清样，以便刻工照样雕刻（即“写样”）。3. 剞劂（jī jué）：本义为雕刻木版用的曲刀和曲凿，后借指雕版刻书、出版。【现代对照】在古代没有现代印刷术与电子排版的条件下，一部医书从著述、校勘、写样到雕版印刷耗时长久且极易受天灾（如火灾）影响，理解这一过程有助于现代学者敬畏古籍流传之不易，并在查阅古籍时注重不同刻本的校勘。

壬辰冬十月。男仲实识

壬辰年（1772年，日本安永元年）冬季十月，儿子仲实谨记。

Recorded in the tenth month of winter in the Renchen year (1772), by his son Zhongshi.

💡 【纪年考证】1. 结合前文火灾发生于“庚辰之春”（1760年），十二年后的“壬辰”（1772年，日本明和九年/安永元年）书成刊行，时间线完全吻合，前后历时十二载方才重新刊行问世。2. “男仲实识”：“男”为古人自称儿子，“识”（zhì）同“志”，意为记述、题记。

Category:中醫

分类：中医

Category: Traditional Chinese Medicine

💡 【文献来源标识】此行为维基文库（Wikisource）等现代数字古籍库抓取页面时附带的网页分类标签，并非《难经古义》古籍原书正文内容。

Base text: Wikisource (public domain)底本：维基文库（公有领域） ↗

Study notes and general health information, not a diagnosis or a treatment plan. Nothing here replaces a doctor, pharmacist or licensed TCM practitioner, and no individual treatment advice is given. Unwell: see a doctor or call 8-1-1 in BC. Emergency: 911.这里是学习笔记和一般健康资料，不是诊断，也不是治疗方案；不替代医生、药剂师或持照中医师，也不提供个人诊疗建议。身体不适请看医生，BC 可拨 8-1-1；紧急情况拨 911。
